JASPAR is an open-access database of curated, non-redundant transcription factor (TF) binding profiles, stored as position frequency matrices (PFMs), as well as deep learning (DL) models, covering multiple species in six taxonomic groups.

You are viewing data from the 11th release (2026) of JASPAR.
